18	//     玩家 50 格内视为可见（WorldEvolution.playerLOS 同款近似）
19	//   - Main.rand / WorldGen.genRand 两条 RNG 流 → 单一 Math.random 流（掷骰顺序
20	//     与原版一致，流不可分）
21	//   - remixWorld（地牢 Y 基准翻转 / 仙女时间窗豁免 / 倒木扫描带翻转）本仓无该
22	//     种子体系 → 恒 false
23	import type { World } from '../World';
24	import type { TileStore } from '../TileStore';
25	import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../data/tiles';
26	
27	/** 倒木 tile（TileID 488 FallenLog；SurfaceDecorPasses place3x2 同一来源） */
28	const FALLEN_LOG = TILE_BY_KEY['v_488_fallen_log'] ?? 0;
29	
30	// ============================================================
31	// ① setFireFlyChance（NPC.cs:94557-94610）
32	// ============================================================
33	/** C# UnifiedRandom.Next(n)（0..n-1） */
34	const next = (n: number): number => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);
35	/** C# UnifiedRandom.Next(lo, hi)（lo..hi-1，上界开区间） */
36	const nextRange = (lo: number, hi: number): number => lo + Math.floor(Math.random() * (hi - lo));
37	
38	/**
39	 * NPC.setFireFlyChance（NPC.cs:94557-94610 1:1）。每夜 StartNight 调用（Main.cs:64706）。
40	 * 三档分布：
41	 *   1/9 的夜（genRand）：fireFlyChance=5..9（萤火虫高发夜）；
42	 *   否则 1/3（genRand）：三值全 999999（当夜萤火虫绝迹）；
43	 *   其余：fireFlyChance=10..59。
44	 * fireFlyFriendly（友好轮夜萤火虫门 :2321）= 高发夜 1..3 / 绝迹夜 999999 / 其余 2..14；
45	 * fireFlyMultiple（附加只概率门 :2329+）同三档。
46	 * 蝴蝶/椿象互斥掷（:94594-94606）：1/3 的夜蝴蝶绝迹 + 椿象 1..13（其中 1/5 椿象也
47	 * 绝迹）；否则椿象绝迹 + 蝴蝶 1..20（其中 1/5 蝴蝶绝迹）。
48	 * 十周年种子两概率减半（下限 1，:94607-94610）。
49	 */
50	export function setFireFlyChance(world: World): void {
51	  // :94566-94585 三档（genRand 两次掷，顺序保持）
52	  if (next(9) === 0) {
53	    world.fireFlyChance = nextRange(5, 10);
54	    world.fireFlyFriendly = nextRange(1, 4);
55	    world.fireFlyMultiple = nextRange(3, 8);
56	  } else if (next(3) === 0) {
57	    world.fireFlyChance = 999999;
58	    world.fireFlyFriendly = 999999;
59	    world.fireFlyMultiple = 999999;
60	  } else {
61	    world.fireFlyChance = nextRange(10, 60);
62	    world.fireFlyFriendly = nextRange(2, 15);
63	    world.fireFlyMultiple = nextRange(6, 30);
64	  }
65	  // :94586-94606 蝴蝶/椿象互斥
66	  if (next(3) === 0) {
67	    world.butterflyChance = 999999;
68	    world.stinkBugChance = nextRange(1, 14);
69	    if (next(5) === 0) world.stinkBugChance = 999999;
70	  } else {
71	    world.stinkBugChance = 999999;
72	    world.butterflyChance = nextRange(1, 21);
73	    if (next(5) === 0) world.butterflyChance = 999999;
74	  }
75	  // :94607-94610 十周年减半
76	  if (world.seedFlags?.tenthAnniversary) {
77	    world.fireFlyChance = Math.max(1, Math.floor(world.fireFlyChance * 0.5));
78	    world.butterflyChance = Math.max(1, Math.floor(world.butterflyChance * 0.5));
79	  }
80	}
81	
82	// ============================================================
83	// ② CultistRitual（CultistRitual.cs 全文）
84	// ============================================================
85	/** CultistRitual.delayStart / respawnDelay / timePerCultist / recheckStart（:7-17） */
86	export const CULTIST_DELAY_START = 86400;
87	export const CULTIST_RESPAWN_DELAY = 43200;
88	export const CULTIST_TIME_PER_CULTIST = 3600;
89	const CULTIST_RECHECK_START = 600;
90	
91	/** CultistRitual 依赖的宿主上下文（Game 侧每帧组装） */
92	export interface CultistHost {
93	  world: World;
94	  /** Main.dungeonX / Main.dungeonY（TrySpawning 锚点） */
95	  dungeonX: number;
96	  dungeonY: number;
97	  /** Main.hardMode */
98	  hardMode: boolean;
99	  /** NPC.downedGolemBoss（石巨人 245） */
100	  downedGolem: boolean;
101	  /** NPC.downedBoss3（骷髅王） */
102	  downedBoss3: boolean;
103	  /** NPC.AnyNPCs(437)：场上已有石碑则不再生成 */
104	  anyTablet: () => boolean;
105	  /** NPC.AnyDanger()（NPC.cs:81040+：月总倒计时/入侵/月事件/旧军/活跃 Boss） */
106	  anyDanger: () => boolean;
107	  /** WorldGen.PlayerLOS 近似（50 格内有玩家视为可见） */
108	  playerLOS: (x: number, y: number) => boolean;
109	  /** 命中落点时的出生回调（NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_WorldEvent(), …, 437)）。
110	   *  返回 false = 出生失败（AnyNPCs(437) 竞态等），原版无返回值，本仓用于日志 */
111	  spawnTablet: (x: number, y: number) => boolean;
112	}
113	
114	/** 运行时静态位（CultistRitual.recheck，:33——不落盘，读档重置 0） */
115	const cultistRecheck = { v: 0 };
116	
117	/** CultistRitual.CultistSlain（:49-53）：delay -= 3600。1.4.5.6 全库无调用点（死码），
118	 *  保留 1:1 语义供台账核对 */
119	export function cultistSlain(world: World): void {
120	  world.cultistDelay -= CULTIST_TIME_PER_CULTIST;
121	}
122	
123	/** CultistRitual.TabletDestroyed（:55-58）：石碑召出 439 的同一帧调用
124	 *  （NPC.cs:38881）→ delay = 43200。不设此账则石碑被消耗后 recheck 周期一到
125	 *  立刻重生（600t ≈ 10s），行为严重偏离原版 */
126	export function cultistTabletDestroyed(world: World): void {
127	  world.cultistDelay = CULTIST_RESPAWN_DELAY;
128	}
129	
130	/** 读图/切世界复位运行时 recheck（原版 recheck 为静态位无显式重置；此处随
131	 *  MysticLogFairiesEvent.WorldClear 同位调用，保持事件态与世界生命周期一致） */
132	export function cultistWorldClear(): void {
133	  cultistRecheck.v = 0;
134	}
135	
136	/** CultistRitual.UpdateTime（:20-47 1:1，Main.cs:64427 每帧）。
137	 *  delay/recheck 均按 dayRate 递减（钳 0）；双零时 recheck=600（AnyDanger 则 ×6）
138	 *  并掷 TrySpawning(dungeonX, dungeonY)。 */
139	export function cultistRitualUpdateTime(world: World, dayRate: number, host: CultistHost): boolean {
140	  world.cultistDelay = Math.max(0, world.cultistDelay - dayRate);
141	  cultistRecheck.v = Math.max(0, cultistRecheck.v - dayRate);
142	  if (world.cultistDelay === 0 && cultistRecheck.v === 0) {
143	    cultistRecheck.v = CULTIST_RECHECK_START;
144	    if (host.anyDanger()) cultistRecheck.v *= 6;
145	    else return cultistTrySpawning(world, host.dungeonX, host.dungeonY, host, false);
146	  }
147	  return false;
148	}
149	
150	/** CultistRitual.TrySpawning（:60-77 1:1）。返回 true = 已出生 437 */
151	export function cultistTrySpawning(world: World, x: number, y: number, host: CultistHost, force: boolean): boolean {
152	  const st = world.store;
153	  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= st.w || y >= st.h) return false;
154	  // !force && 玩家看得见锚点两侧 → 拒（WorldGen.PlayerLOS 近似）
155	  if (!force && (host.playerLOS(x - 6, y) || host.playerLOS(x + 6, y))) return false;
156	  if (!cultistCheckRitual(world, st, x, y, host, force)) return false;
157	  // NPC.NewNPC(..., x*16+8, (y-4)*16-8, 437)
158	  return host.spawnTablet(x * 16 + 8, (y - 4) * 16 - 8);
159	}
160	
161	/** CultistRitual.CheckRitual（:79-104 1:1） */
162	function cultistCheckRitual(world: World, st: TileStore, x: number, y: number,
163	  host: CultistHost, force: boolean): boolean {
164	  if (!force && (world.cultistDelay !== 0 || !host.hardMode || !host.downedGolem || !host.downedBoss3)) {
165	    return false;
166	  }
167	  // 锚点上方 7 格须在界内且非实心（石碑悬浮于地牢入口上空）
168	  if (y < 7 || st.isSolid(x, y - 7)) return false;
169	  if (!force && host.anyTablet()) return false;   // NPC.AnyNPCs(437)
170	  // CheckFloor：锚点中心向下扫四根柱，须凑齐 4 个"实心/平台且上方净空"落点
171	  const cx = x * 16 + 8;
172	  const cy = y * 16 - 64 - 8 - 27;
173	  return cultistCheckFloor(st, cx, cy);
174	}
175	
176	/** CultistRitual.CheckFloor（:106-135 1:1）：i∈{-5,-3,1,3,5}（跳过 ±1）→
177	 *  柱 x = tileX+i*2，y 自 tileY-5 向下扫至 +11，首个"实心/平台 且
178	 *  (上方 3 行三列净空 或 本柱净空+两邻柱上 2 行净空)"的点计入；凑不齐 4 点 → false */
179	export function cultistCheckFloor(st: TileStore, centerX: number, centerY: number): boolean {
180	  const tx = Math.floor(centerX / 16);
181	  const ty = Math.floor(centerY / 16);
182	  let found = 0;
183	  for (let i = -5; i <= 5; i += 2) {
184	    if (i === -1 || i === 1) continue;
185	    for (let j = -5; j < 12; j++) {
186	      const x = tx + i * 2;
187	      const y = ty + j;
188	      if ((st.isSolid(x, y) || st.isPlatform(x, y))
189	        && (!solidRect(st, x - 1, x + 1, y - 3, y - 1)
190	          || (!solidRect(st, x, x, y - 3, y - 1)
191	            && !solidRect(st, x + 1, x + 1, y - 3, y - 2)
192	            && !solidRect(st, x - 1, x - 1, y - 3, y - 2)))) {
193	        found++;
194	        break;
195	      }
196	    }
197	  }
198	  return found === 4;
199	}
200	
201	// ============================================================
202	// ③ MysticLogFairiesEvent（MysticLogFairiesEvent.cs 全文）
203	// ============================================================
204	/** 仙女事件宿主上下文（Game 侧组装） */
205	export interface FairyHost {
206	  world: World;
207	  /** Main.maxRaining（>0 = 雨天，压制仙女） */
208	  maxRaining: number;
209	  /** Main.bloodMoon */
210	  bloodMoon: boolean;
211	  /** NPC.MoonLordCountdown（>0 压制） */
212	  moonLordCountdown: number;
213	  /** Main.snowMoon / Main.pumpkinMoon */
214	  snowMoon: boolean;
215	  pumpkinMoon: boolean;
216	  /** Main.invasionType（>0 压制） */
217	  invasionType: number;
218	  /** WorldGen.PlayerLOS 近似 */
219	  playerLOS: (x: number, y: number) => boolean;
220	  /** NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_WorldEvent(), …, id)——出生回调 */
221	  spawnFairy: (id: number, x: number, y: number) => void;
222	}
223	
224	/** 倒木桩锚点（tile 坐标，已折算 frameX/frameY 到整件左上角） */
225	interface StumpCoord { x: number; y: number; }
226	
227	/** DELAY_BETWEEN_ATTEMPTS（:22） */
228	const FAIRY_DELAY_BETWEEN_ATTEMPTS = 60;
229	
230	/** MysticLogFairiesEvent 事件态（原版为 WorldGen.mysticLogsEvent 实例） */
231	export interface MysticLogState {
232	  _canSpawnFairies: boolean;
233	  _delayUntilNextAttempt: number;
234	  _stumpCoords: StumpCoord[];
235	  /** WorldClear（:24-32） */
236	  worldClear(): void;
237	  /** StartWorld（:34-39，WorldGen.cs:3303 OnWorldLoad）：读图/新世界扫一次倒木 */
238	  startWorld(host: FairyHost): void;
239	  /** StartNight（:41-48，Main.cs:64710）：开闸 + 重扫倒木 */
240	  startNight(host: FairyHost): void;
241	  /** FallenLogDestroyed（:152-159，WorldGen.cs:49866 KillTile type 488）：重扫 */
242	  fallenLogDestroyed(host: FairyHost): void;
243	  /** UpdateTime（:50-62，Main.cs:64433 每帧） */
244	  updateTime(world: World, dayRate: number, host: FairyHost): void;
245	  /** TrySpawningFairies（:64-119 1:1） */
246	  trySpawningFairies(world: World, dayRate: number, host: FairyHost): void;
247	  /** ScanWholeOverworldForLogs（:161-189 1:1，非 remix） */
248	  scanWholeOverworldForLogs(st: TileStore, worldSurface: number): void;
249	}
250	
251	/** 单例事件态（运行时态不落盘，WorldClear 于读图重置——WorldGen.cs:6958） */
252	export const mysticLogs: MysticLogState = {
253	  _canSpawnFairies: false,
254	  _delayUntilNextAttempt: 0,
255	  _stumpCoords: [],
256	
257	  worldClear(): void {
258	    this._canSpawnFairies = false;
259	    this._delayUntilNextAttempt = 0;
260	    this._stumpCoords.length = 0;
261	  },
262	
263	  startWorld(host: FairyHost): void {
264	    this.scanWholeOverworldForLogs(host.world.store, host.world.groundLevel);
265	  },
266	
267	  startNight(host: FairyHost): void {
268	    this._canSpawnFairies = true;
269	    this._delayUntilNextAttempt = 0;
270	    this.scanWholeOverworldForLogs(host.world.store, host.world.groundLevel);
271	  },
272	
273	  fallenLogDestroyed(host: FairyHost): void {
274	    this.scanWholeOverworldForLogs(host.world.store, host.world.groundLevel);
275	  },
276	
277	  /** 夜窗内按 dayRate 递减尝试倒计时，到 0 掷一次 TrySpawningFairies 并重置 60 */
278	  updateTime(world: World, dayRate: number, host: FairyHost): void {
279	    if (!this._canSpawnFairies || !isAGoodTime(world)) return;
280	    this._delayUntilNextAttempt = Math.max(0, this._delayUntilNextAttempt - dayRate);
281	    if (this._delayUntilNextAttempt === 0) {
282	      this._delayUntilNextAttempt = FAIRY_DELAY_BETWEEN_ATTEMPTS;
283	      this.trySpawningFairies(world, dayRate, host);
284	    }
285	  },
286	
287	  trySpawningFairies(world: World, dayRate: number, host: FairyHost): void {
288	    // :66-69 压制门：雨/血月/月总倒计时/霜月/南瓜月/入侵/无倒木
289	    if (host.maxRaining > 0 || host.bloodMoon || host.moonLordCountdown > 0
290	      || host.snowMoon || host.pumpkinMoon || host.invasionType > 0
291	      || this._stumpCoords.length === 0) {
292	      return;
293	    }
294	    // :71-80 概率门：dayRate 次掷 1/oneOverSpawnChance（满月/新月夜 60，其余 180）
295	    const oneOverSpawnChance = getOneOverSpawnChance(world);
296	    let hit = false;
297	    for (let i = 0; i < dayRate; i++) {
298	      if (next(oneOverSpawnChance) === 0) { hit = true; break; }
299	    }
300	    if (!hit) return;
301	    // :82-90 随机取一桩；出生点 = 桩左上角世界坐标 (x*16+24, y*16+24) 上移 50px；
302	    // 玩家看得见 → 放弃
303	    const p = this._stumpCoords[next(this._stumpCoords.length)];
304	    const vx = p.x * 16 + 24;
305	    const vy = p.y * 16 + 24 - 50;
306	    if (host.playerLOS(p.x, p.y)) return;
307	    // :92-94 数量：1..3 只，1/7 再 +1
308	    let count = nextRange(1, 4);
309	    if (next(7) === 0) count++;
310	    // :96-110 每只独立掷 585/584/583（十周年 3/4 概率强制 583）
311	    for (let j = 0; j < count; j++) {
312	      let id = [585, 584, 583][next(3)];
313	      if (world.seedFlags?.tenthAnniversary && next(4) !== 0) id = 583;
314	      host.spawnFairy(id, Math.floor(vx), Math.floor(vy));
315	    }
316	    // :112 一夜只放一批
317	    this._canSpawnFairies = false;
318	  },
319	
320	  /** x∈[100, maxTilesX-100) 步 3、y 自 worldSurface-10 降到 100 步 2，
321	   *  活跃 tile 488 且无液体 → 计入（帧偏移折算整件左上角） */
322	  scanWholeOverworldForLogs(st: TileStore, worldSurface: number): void {
323	    this._stumpCoords.length = 0;
324	    if (!FALLEN_LOG) return;   // tile 表缺登记（理论不可达，防御）
325	    const yTop = Math.floor(worldSurface) - 10;
326	    const yBot = 100;
327	    const xEnd = st.w - 100;
328	    for (let x = 100; x < xEnd; x += 3) {
329	      for (let y = yTop; y >= yBot; y -= 2) {
330	        if (y < 0 || y >= st.h) continue;
331	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
332	        if (!st.flags[i] || st.type[i] !== FALLEN_LOG || st.liquid[i] !== 0) continue;
333	        // GetStumpTopLeft（:191-199）：格坐标减 frameX/18、frameY/18 → 整件左上角
334	        this._stumpCoords.push({
335	          x: x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18),
336	          y: y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 18),
337	        });
338	      }
339	    }
340	  },
341	};
342	
343	/** IsAGoodTime（:121-136 1:1，非 remix）：夜间且 Main.time∈[6480, 25920]
344	 *  （≈9:18PM-2:42AM）。Main.time 夜段 = 夜内进度 × 32400 */
345	function isAGoodTime(world: World): boolean {
346	  if (world.clock.isDay) return false;
347	  const nightFrac = (((world.clock.timeOfDay - 0.8125) % 1) + 1) % 1 / 0.375;
348	  const time = nightFrac * 32400;
349	  return time >= 6480.0000965595245 && time <= 25920.000386238098;
350	}
351	
352	/** GetOneOverSpawnChance（:201-208 1:1）：满月/新月 3600，其余 10800；/60 */
353	function getOneOverSpawnChance(world: World): number {
354	  const full = world.clock.moonPhase === 0;
355	  const empty = world.clock.moonPhase === 4;
356	  return (full || empty ? 3600 : 10800) / 60;
357	}
358	
359	// ---- 共享 tile 判定（TileStore.isSolid 同语义：界内 + 未致动 + 实心） ----
360	
361	/** 矩形内存在实心格（Collision.SolidTiles 消费面） */
362	function solidRect(st: TileStore, x0: number, x1: number, y0: number, y1: number): boolean {
363	  for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
364	    for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
365	      if (st.isSolid(x, y)) return true;
366	    }
367	  }
368	  return false;
369	}
370	

```

using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
using Terraria.DataStructures;
using Terraria.ID;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Events;

public class CultistRitual
{
	public const int delayStart = 86400;

	public const int respawnDelay = 43200;

	private const int timePerCultist = 3600;

	private const int recheckStart = 600;

	public static int delay;

	public static int recheck;

	public static void UpdateTime()
	{
		if (Main.netMode == 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		delay -= Main.dayRate;
		if (delay < 0)
		{
			delay = 0;
		}
		recheck -= Main.dayRate;
		if (recheck < 0)
		{
			recheck = 0;
		}
		if (delay == 0 && recheck == 0)
		{
			recheck = 600;
			if (NPC.AnyDanger())
			{
				recheck *= 6;
			}
			else
			{
				TrySpawning(Main.dungeonX, Main.dungeonY);
			}
		}
	}

	public static void CultistSlain()
	{
		delay -= 3600;
	}

	public static void TabletDestroyed()
	{
		delay = 43200;
	}

	public static bool TrySpawning(int x, int y, bool force = false)
	{
		if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= Main.maxTilesX || y >= Main.maxTilesY)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!force && (WorldGen.PlayerLOS(x - 6, y) || WorldGen.PlayerLOS(x + 6, y)))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!CheckRitual(x, y, force))
		{
			return false;
		}
		NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_WorldEvent(), x * 16 + 8, (y - 4) * 16 - 8, 437);
		return true;
	}

	private static bool CheckRitual(int x, int y, bool force = false)
	{
		if (!force && (delay != 0 || !Main.hardMode || !NPC.downedGolemBoss || !NPC.downedBoss3))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (y < 7 || WorldGen.SolidTile(Main.tile[x, y - 7]))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!force && NPC.AnyNPCs(437))
		{
			return false;
		}
		Vector2 center = new Vector2(x * 16 + 8, y * 16 - 64 - 8 - 27);
		Point[] spawnPoints = null;
		if (!CheckFloor(center, out spawnPoints))
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public static bool CheckFloor(Vector2 Center, out Point[] spawnPoints)
	{
		Point[] array = new Point[4];
		int num = 0;
		Point point = Center.ToTileCoordinates();
		for (int i = -5; i <= 5; i += 2)
		{
			if (i == -1 || i == 1)
			{
				continue;
			}
			for (int j = -5; j < 12; j++)
			{
				int num2 = point.X + i * 2;
				int num3 = point.Y + j;
				if ((WorldGen.SolidTile(num2, num3) || TileID.Sets.Platforms[Framing.GetTileSafely(num2, num3).type]) && (!Collision.SolidTiles(num2 - 1, num2 + 1, num3 - 3, num3 - 1) || (!Collision.SolidTiles(num2, num2, num3 - 3, num3 - 1) && !Collision.SolidTiles(num2 + 1, num2 + 1, num3 - 3, num3 - 2) && !Collision.SolidTiles(num2 - 1, num2 - 1, num3 - 3, num3 - 2))))
				{
					array[num++] = new Point(num2, num3);
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (num != 4)
		{
			spawnPoints = null;
			return false;
		}
		spawnPoints = array;
		return true;
	}

	public static bool CheckFloor2(Vector2 Center, out Point[] spawnPoints)
	{
		Point[] array = new Point[2];
		int num = 0;
		Point point = Center.ToTileCoordinates();
		for (int i = -3; i <= 3; i += 2)
		{
			if (i == -1 || i == 1)
			{
				continue;
			}
			for (int j = -5; j < 12; j++)
			{
				int num2 = point.X + i * 2;
				int num3 = point.Y + j;
				if ((WorldGen.SolidTile(num2, num3) || TileID.Sets.Platforms[Framing.GetTileSafely(num2, num3).type]) && (!Collision.SolidTiles(num2 - 1, num2 + 1, num3 - 3, num3 - 1) || (!Collision.SolidTiles(num2, num2, num3 - 3, num3 - 1) && !Collision.SolidTiles(num2 + 1, num2 + 1, num3 - 3, num3 - 2) && !Collision.SolidTiles(num2 - 1, num2 - 1, num3 - 3, num3 - 2))))
				{
					array[num++] = new Point(num2, num3);
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (num != 2)
		{
			spawnPoints = null;
			return false;
		}
		spawnPoints = array;
		return true;
	}
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
using Terraria.DataStructures;
using Terraria.Enums;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Events;

public class MysticLogFairiesEvent
{
	private bool _canSpawnFairies;

	private int _delayUntilNextAttempt;

	private const int DELAY_BETWEEN_ATTEMPTS = 60;

	private List<Point> _stumpCoords = new List<Point>();

	public void WorldClear()
	{
		_canSpawnFairies = false;
		_delayUntilNextAttempt = 0;
		_stumpCoords.Clear();
	}

	public void StartWorld()
	{
		if (Main.netMode != 1)
		{
			ScanWholeOverworldForLogs();
		}
	}

	public void StartNight()
	{
		if (Main.netMode != 1)
		{
			_canSpawnFairies = true;
			_delayUntilNextAttempt = 0;
			ScanWholeOverworldForLogs();
		}
	}

	public void UpdateTime()
	{
		if (Main.netMode != 1 && _canSpawnFairies && IsAGoodTime())
		{
			_delayUntilNextAttempt = Math.Max(0, _delayUntilNextAttempt - Main.dayRate);
			if (_delayUntilNextAttempt == 0)
			{
				_delayUntilNextAttempt = 60;
				TrySpawningFairies();
			}
		}
	}

	private bool IsAGoodTime()
	{
		if (Main.dayTime)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!Main.remixWorld)
		{
			if (Main.time < 6480.0000965595245)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Main.time > 25920.000386238098)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	private void TrySpawningFairies()
	{
		if (Main.maxRaining > 0f || Main.bloodMoon || NPC.MoonLordCountdown > 0 || Main.snowMoon || Main.pumpkinMoon || Main.invasionType > 0 || _stumpCoords.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		int oneOverSpawnChance = GetOneOverSpawnChance();
		bool flag = false;
		for (int i = 0; i < Main.dayRate; i++)
		{
			if (Main.rand.Next(oneOverSpawnChance) == 0)
			{
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (!flag)
		{
			return;
		}
		int index = Main.rand.Next(_stumpCoords.Count);
		Point p = _stumpCoords[index];
		Vector2 vector = p.ToWorldCoordinates(24f);
		vector.Y -= 50f;
		if (WorldGen.PlayerLOS(p.X, p.Y))
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = Main.rand.Next(1, 4);
		if (Main.rand.Next(7) == 0)
		{
			num++;
		}
		int num2 = Utils.SelectRandom(Main.rand, new short[3] { 585, 584, 583 });
		for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
		{
			num2 = Utils.SelectRandom(Main.rand, new short[3] { 585, 584, 583 });
			if (Main.tenthAnniversaryWorld && Main.rand.Next(4) != 0)
			{
				num2 = 583;
			}
			int num3 = NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_WorldEvent(), (int)vector.X, (int)vector.Y, num2);
			if (Main.netMode == 2 && num3 < Main.maxNPCs)
			{
				NetMessage.SendData(23, -1, -1, null, num3);
			}
		}
		_canSpawnFairies = false;
	}

	public void FallenLogDestroyed()
	{
		if (Main.netMode != 1)
		{
			ScanWholeOverworldForLogs();
		}
	}

	private void ScanWholeOverworldForLogs()
	{
		_stumpCoords.Clear();
		NPC.Spawner.fairyLog = false;
		int num = (int)Main.worldSurface - 10;
		int num2 = 100;
		int num3 = Main.maxTilesX - 100;
		if (Main.remixWorld)
		{
			num = Main.maxTilesY - 350;
			num2 = (int)Main.rockLayer;
		}
		int num4 = 3;
		int num5 = 2;
		List<Point> list = new List<Point>();
		for (int i = 100; i < num3; i += num4)
		{
			for (int num6 = num; num6 >= num2; num6 -= num5)
			{
				Tile tile = Main.tile[i, num6];
				if (tile.active() && tile.type == 488 && tile.liquid == 0)
				{
					list.Add(new Point(i, num6));
					NPC.Spawner.fairyLog = true;
				}
			}
		}
		foreach (Point item in list)
		{
			_stumpCoords.Add(GetStumpTopLeft(item));
		}
	}

	private Point GetStumpTopLeft(Point stumpRandomPoint)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[stumpRandomPoint.X, stumpRandomPoint.Y];
		Point result = stumpRandomPoint;
		result.X -= tile.frameX / 18;
		result.Y -= tile.frameY / 18;
		return result;
	}

	private int GetOneOverSpawnChance()
	{
		int num = 1;
		MoonPhase moonPhase = Main.GetMoonPhase();
		num = ((moonPhase != MoonPhase.Full && moonPhase != MoonPhase.Empty) ? 10800 : 3600);
		return num / 60;
	}
}

```

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Terraria.DataStructures;
using Terraria.ID;
using Terraria.Localization;

namespace Terraria.GameContent;

public abstract class ConditionalDialogue
{
	public static class ItemGroups
	{
		public static RecipeGroup Ore = new RecipeGroup("RecipeGroups.Ore", 699, 12, 11, 700, 14, 701, 13, 702);

		public static RecipeGroup Bars = new RecipeGroup("RecipeGroups.Bar", 703, 20, 22, 704, 21, 705, 19, 706);

		public static RecipeGroup Anvils = new RecipeGroup("ItemName.IronAnvil", 35, 716);

		public static RecipeGroup Whips = new RecipeGroup("RecipeGroups.Whip");

		public static RecipeGroup Mounts = new RecipeGroup("RecipeGroups.Mount");

		internal static void PostSetupContent()
		{
			foreach (Item value in ContentSamples.ItemsByType.Values)
			{
				if (ProjectileID.Sets.IsAWhip[value.shoot])
				{
					Whips.Add(value.type);
				}
			}
			foreach (Item value2 in ContentSamples.ItemsByType.Values)
			{
				if (value2.mountType != -1)
				{
					Mounts.Add(value2.type);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private class FreeCakeDialogue : ConditionalDialogue
	{
		public FreeCakeDialogue()
			: base((NPC _) => NPC.freeCake)
		{
		}

		public override string GetChatAndClearCondition(NPC npc)
		{
			NPC.freeCake = false;
			NetMessage.SendData(51, -1, -1, null, 0, 10f);
			Item item = new Item();
			item.SetDefaults(3750);
			Main.LocalPlayer.QuickSpawnItem(new EntitySource_Gift(npc), item, GetItemSettings.GiftRecieved);
			return Language.GetTextValue("PartyGirlSpecialText.Cake" + Main.rand.Next(1, 4));
		}
	}

	private static List<ConditionalDialogue>[] _registry = new List<ConditionalDialogue>[NPCID.Count];

	public readonly Predicate<NPC> ConditionsMet;

	public bool ShowIndicator { get; private set; }

	private static void Register(int npcType, ConditionalDialogue dialogue)
	{
		List<ConditionalDialogue> list = _registry[npcType];
		if (list == null)
		{
			list = (_registry[npcType] = new List<ConditionalDialogue>());
		}
		list.Add(dialogue);
	}

	public static bool TryGetPendingDialogue(NPC npc, out ConditionalDialogue dialogue)
	{
		dialogue = null;
		List<ConditionalDialogue> list = _registry[npc.type];
		if (list == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		foreach (ConditionalDialogue item in list)
		{
			if (item.ConditionsMet(npc))
			{
				dialogue = item;
				return true;
			}

**Main.cs UpdateTime_StartNight order:**
1. fastForwardTimeToDusk / UpdateTimeRate
2. moondialCooldown--
3. !isThereAWorldSurface → spawnMeteor=false
4. **NPC.ResetBadgerHatTime()** ← first
5. **NPC.freeCake = false** ← second
6. Star.NightSetup()
7. **NPC.setFireFlyChance()**
8. **BirthdayParty.CheckNight()**
9. **LanternNight.CheckNight()**
10. **WorldGen.mysticLogsEvent.StartNight()**
11. WorldGen.prioritizedTownNPCType = 0
12. checkForSpawns = 0
13. rand.Next(50)==0 && downedBoss2 → spawnMeteor = true
14. LanternNight.LanternsUp → stopEvents = true
15. eclipse && netMode!=1 → achievement; eclipse=false
16. achievement(0)
17. if !IsFastForwardingTime() && !stopEvents: eye of cthulhu roll etc. (blood moon further below at ~64750+)

So the order in vanilla: ResetBadgerHatTime → freeCake=false → Star.NightSetup → setFireFlyChance → BirthdayParty.CheckNight → LanternNight.CheckNight → mysticLogsEvent.StartNight → ... → blood moon roll (further down, after the eye/mech rolls).

235	export function rlePairs(values: ArrayLike<number>): Uint8Array {
236	  const out = new ByteWriter();
237	  const n = values.length;
238	  let i = 0;
239	  while (i < n) {
240	    const v = values[i];
241	    let run = 1;
242	    while (i + run < n && values[i + run] === v && run < 0x7fffffff) run++;
243	    out.varint(run - 1);
244	    out.varint(v);
245	    i += run;
246	  }
247	  return out.bytes();
248	}
249	
250	/** RLE tiles。stableMap 非空时逐元素映射为稳定 id(v3 存档路径) */
251	export function rleTiles(st: { type: Uint16Array; frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array }, stableMap?: Uint16Array): Uint8Array {
252	  const out = new ByteWriter();
253	  const n = st.type.length;
254	  let i = 0;
255	  while (i < n) {
256	    const raw = st.type[i];
257	    const t = stableMap ? (raw === 0 ? 0 : stableMap[raw]) : raw;
258	    const fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
259	    let run = 1;
260	    // 游程比较必须 raw 对 raw（t 是映射后的稳定 id，与原始 id 比较恒不等 →
261	    // 自定义 tile（stable≠internal）游程全断，4200x1200 规则地形 17B 膨胀到 21MB）
262	    while (i + run < n && st.type[i + run] === raw && st.frameX[i + run] === fx && st.frameY[i + run] === fy) run++;
263	    out.varint(run - 1);
264	    out.varint(t);
265	    if (t !== 0) {
266	      out.varint(fx);
267	      out.varint(fy);
268	    }
269	    i += run;
270	  }
271	  return out.bytes();
272	}
273

275	 * worker=副本,净化不影响游戏内状态,存档字节一致) */
276	export function serializeSave(
277	  st: SaveStoreView, meta: SaveMeta,

101	  /** 附魔日晷/月晷快进（Main.fastForwardTimeToDawn/Dusk + sundialCooldown/moondialCooldown，
102	   *  Main.cs:6240-6268 Sundialing/Moondialing：使用置旗 + 冷却 8 天；快进中 dayRate=60。
103	   *  清旗点 = UpdateTime_StartDay :64844-64847（dawn）/ StartNight :64691-64694（dusk），
104	   *  冷却递减 = StartDay :64885-64887（sundial）/ StartNight :64695-64697（moondial），
105	   *  血月清零 = StartNight :64892-64893。随世界存档持久化（WorldFile.cs:1311/1342/1416-1417） */
106	  fastForwardTimeToDawn = false;
107	  fastForwardTimeToDusk = false;
108	  sundialCooldown = 0;
109	  moondialCooldown = 0;
110	  /** 全员熟睡旗（CurrentFrameFlags.SleepingPlayersCount==ActivePlayersCount && >0，
111	   *  Main.cs:6280-6285——Game 每 tick 下发；单人 = 本地玩家 sleeping && timeSleeping>=120） */
112	  allSleeping = false;
113	  /** IsFastForwardingTime（Main.cs:6230-6238）——事件 roll 压制门共用 */
114	  isFastForwardingTime(): boolean { return this.fastForwardTimeToDawn || this.fastForwardTimeToDusk; }
115	  /** 专家世界（Main.cs:2775-2777：GameMode==1/2 → Difficulty≥Expert；旅程 3 不算） */
116	  get isExpert(): boolean { return this.difficulty === 1 || this.difficulty === 2; }
117	  /** 大师世界（Main.cs:2779-2780：仅 GameMode==2） */
118	  get isMaster(): boolean { return this.difficulty === 2; }
119	  /** 旅程世界（Main.cs:2696 IsJourneyMode：GameMode==3） */
120	  get isJourney(): boolean { return this.difficulty === 3; }
121	  /** 当前 dayRate（Main.cs:6270-6294 UpdateTimeRate 1:1）：快进恒 60；
122	   *  否则冻结 0 / TargetTimeRate × 全员睡 5。时钟/天气/税计时/旅行商概率全链消费 */
123	  dayRate(): number {
124	    if (this.isFastForwardingTime()) return 60;
125	    if (this.journeyFreezeTime) return 0;
126	    return this.journeyTimeRate * (this.allSleeping ? 5 : 1);
127	  }
128	  /** 时间推进倍率（Main.cs:6278-6290 UpdateTimeRate：冻结 0 / 否则 TargetTimeRate；gameMenu 恒 1）
129	   *  ——不含快进/睡觉两档的旧入口，保留给不参与 dayRate 链的调用方 */

162	export function loadSaveData(data: SaveData, onTilesProgress?: (p: number) => void): { world: World; player: SaveData['player']; data: SaveData } {
163	  // 物品 id 还原(箱子/背包/储物就地重写):v3+ 走稳定 id;v2 旧档直读(接受破损政策)。
164	  // v4 起 armor/dye/trash/misc/loadouts 也是稳定 id;v3 恰为裸内部下标——用迁移锚
165	  // 快照(LEGACY_KEY_OF_INTERNAL_V3)反查 key 再转 vi_/原定义(本地物品退役兼容层)
166	  const v3 = (data.version ?? 1) >= 3;
167	  const v4 = (data.version ?? 1) >= 4;
168	  const mapItem = (it: SavedItemT | null): SavedItemT | null =>
169	    it && v3 ? { ...it, id: it.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE.get(it.id) ?? 0 } : it;
170	  const mapLegacySlot = (it: SavedItemT | null): SavedItemT | null => {
171	    if (!it) return it;
172	    if (v4) return mapItem(it);
173	    if (!v3) return it; // v2 及更早:裸下标直读
174	    // v3:armor/dye/trash/misc/loadouts 段 = 保存时构建的裸内部下标
175	    const key = LEGACY_KEY_OF_INTERNAL_V3[it.id];
176	    if (key === undefined) return it;
177	    if (/^vi_(\d+)_/.test(key)) {
178	      // vi_ 键:键本身仍在注册表(蛇形/驼峰双注册内容等价),直接反查内部 id。
179	      // ★ 不能经 ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[裸下标] —— 本地 184 条删除后下标整体左移
180	      const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[key];
181	      return id !== undefined ? { ...it, id } : it;
182	    }
183	    // 本地键:退役映射(→vi_);纯自创删除键→归 0
184	    const viId = viIdOfRetired(key);
185	    return { ...it, id: viId > 0 ? viId : 0 };
186	  };
187	  // v3 稳定 id → 内部 id(含 locked 透传);★曾未接线直接用 data.chests——
188	  // 现有物品 stable≡internal 掩盖,自定义物品进箱即错
189	  const v3Chests = v3
190	    ? data.chests.map((c) => ({ x: c.x, y: c.y, locked: c.locked, items: c.items.map(mapItem) }))
191	    : data.chests;
192	  if (v3 && data.player) {
193	    data.player = {
194	      ...data.player,
195	      inventory: data.player.inventory.map(mapItem),
196	      banks: data.player.banks?.map((b) => b.map(mapItem)),
197	      armor: data.player.armor?.map(mapLegacySlot),
198	      dye: data.player.dye?.map(mapLegacySlot),
199	      trash: mapLegacySlot(data.player.trash ?? null),
200	      miscEquips: data.player.miscEquips?.map(mapLegacySlot),
201	      miscDyes: data.player.miscDyes?.map(mapLegacySlot),
202	      loadouts: data.player.loadouts?.map((l) => ({
203	        armor: l.armor.map(mapLegacySlot),
204	        dye: l.dye.map(mapLegacySlot),
205	      })),
206	    };
207	  }
208	  const world = new WorldClass(data.header.width, data.header.height, data.header.seed, data.header.name);
209	  world.spawnX = data.header.spawn[0];
210	  world.spawnY = data.header.spawn[1];
211	  world.groundLevel = data.header.groundLevel;
212	  world.rockLevel = data.header.rockLevel;
213	  // 旧档缺 lavaLine（原版 UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200）
214	  world.lavaLine = data.header.lavaLine ?? data.header.height - 200;
215	  if (data.header.treeX) world.treeX = data.header.treeX;
216	  if (data.header.treeStyle) world.treeStyle = data.header.treeStyle;
217	  if (data.header.treeTops?.length) world.treeTops = data.header.treeTops;
218	  if (data.header.crimson !== undefined) world.crimson = data.header.crimson;
219	  if (data.header.dungeonX !== undefined) world.dungeonX = data.header.dungeonX;
220	  // dungeonY 缺省（旧档/导入）回退地表线：CheckToSpawnDungeonEnemies 的 pY<dungeonY+40 门槛近似成立
221	  if (data.header.dungeonY !== undefined && data.header.dungeonY > 0) world.dungeonY = data.header.dungeonY;
222	  else world.dungeonY = world.groundLevel;
223	  if (data.header.jungleX !== undefined) world.jungleX = data.header.jungleX;
224	  world.clock.timeOfDay = data.clock.timeOfDay;
225	  world.clock.dayCount = data.clock.dayCount;
226	  // 原版 header _temp 三件套（WF:1276-1278）;旧档缺省=无血月/无日食/满月
227	  if (data.clock.bloodMoon !== undefined) world.clock.bloodMoon = data.clock.bloodMoon;
228	  if (data.clock.eclipse !== undefined) world.clock.eclipse = data.clock.eclipse;
229	  if (data.clock.moonPhase !== undefined) world.clock.moonPhase = data.clock.moonPhase;
230	  if (data.header.moonType !== undefined) world.moonType = data.header.moonType;

**Important finding on defaults**: In vanilla, the five NPC statics default to **0** (`public static int fireFlyChance = 0;` etc.). And they are NOT in WorldFile (no persistence). They're set by `setFireFlyChance()` on the first StartNight. 

Our repo defaults: fireFlyChance=30, fireFlyFriendly=5, fireFlyMultiple=15, butterflyChance=10, stinkBugChance=999999.

Difference: In vanilla, before the first night (i.e., a freshly loaded world at day time), `fireFlyChance == 0` → `Main.rand.Next(0) == 0` — wait, `Main.rand.Next(0)` in C# returns 0 always? UnifiedRandom.Next(0) returns 0 (maxValue <= 0 returns 0). So `Next(0) == 0` is TRUE → fireflies would ALWAYS spawn during the initial day?! Hmm wait, but the firefly consumers at :2321/:2381/:2394 require `!Main.dayTime` for fireflies and `Main.dayTime` for butterflies/stinkbugs. So during the initial day (before any night), butterflyChance=0 → Next(0)==0 → true → butterflies spawn at max rate. Hmm, that seems odd but it's vanilla behavior. Actually wait — when a new world is created, does StartNight get called before gameplay? No. World starts at 8:15 AM (13500 time). So the first day has butterflyChance=0 → always-spawn butterflies. Is that really vanilla behavior? Hmm. Let me think... In practice, in vanilla 1.4, do butterflies spawn on day 1? I believe critters do spawn immediately on a new world. Yes — you see butterflies on day 1 in a new Terraria world. OK.

So our defaults (30/5/15/10/999999) differ from vanilla's pre-first-night zeros: our initial day has butterflyChance=10 (1/10 per attempt) vs vanilla's 0 (=always). And fireFlyChance=30 vs vanilla 0 — but fireflies only spawn at night, and by the first night setFireFlyChance has run. So the only observable difference is **day-1 butterflies/stinkbugs**: vanilla `butterflyChance=0` → `Next(0)==0` → always spawn butterflies on day 1; ours 1/10 per attempt. Also stinkBugChance: vanilla 0 → always, ours 999999 → never. Hmm, interesting. But wait — is that vanilla's real behavior, or does setFireFlyChance get called at world load? Let me check whether there's a call to setFireFlyChance somewhere else (e.g., in Main.LoadWorld or WorldGen). Let me grep for setFireFlyChance call sites in vanilla.

And our default 30/5/15/10/999999 for pre-first-night only matters for a NEW world in our repo before the first dusk — vanilla would have a fresh roll from the load path... wait, does our repo call setFireFlyChance on new world creation? So a brand-new world in our repo starts with 30/5/15/10/999999 for the first day+night until dusk — but dusk comes ~11.5 in-game hours after 8:15 AM start... so the first NIGHT already re-rolls. Only the first DAY uses the defaults (daytime critters: butterfly 1/10, stinkbug never). Vanilla first day: fresh roll from WF:770 (e.g., butterfly 1/20 or 绝迹). So both are "arbitrary values on day 1" — vanilla random, ours fixed representative. Truly minor. 

BUT the more notable asymmetry: **after a save/load mid-day in vanilla, the daytime critter chances get RE-ROLLED** — which means e.g. a butterfly-绝迹 day could become a butterfly day after reload. Ours keeps it. Truly cosmetic. I'll fold this into one low finding: "vanilla re-rolls at world load (WF:770); ours persists and never re-rolls on load — documented deviation, plus defaults differ from vanilla's static 0s".

Hmm, actually — hold on. Is persistence maybe WORSE than re-roll for the "绝迹夜" case? If a night rolls 999999 (绝迹) and you save at night and reload, vanilla re-rolls → could get fireflies. Ours stays 绝迹. Minor.

Excellent — very useful:

**PlayerLOS (WorldGen.cs:69500-69515)**: NOT a line-of-sight check at all! It's a rectangle intersection: the point's 16×16 rect vs a rect centered on the player with size `sWidth*1.2 × sHeight*1.2` (screen size × 1.2). So it's a "is the point on the player's (expanded) screen" check. Screen is e.g. 1920×1080 → the box is 2304×1296 px = 144×81 tiles. So PlayerLOS ≈ "within ~1.2 screens of the player" — NOT "within 50 tiles". 

Our approximation: `|Δx| < 50 tiles && |Δy| < 40 tiles` = a 100×80 tile box. Vanilla's box: (screenW*1.2)/16 × (screenH*1.2)/16 — for 1920×1080 that's 144×81 tiles, centered. So our box (100×80) is SMALLER than vanilla's (144×81) → we refuse LESS often than vanilla → tablets/fairies may spawn closer to the player than vanilla would allow (potentially on-screen!). 

Direction check: PlayerLOS true → refuse spawn. Vanilla: true when the point is within the 1.2× screen box. Ours: true when within 50×40 tile box. For a 1920×1080 screen: vanilla blocks spawns within ±72 tiles X / ±40.5 tiles Y. Ours blocks within ±50 X / ±40 Y. So ours blocks LESS (±50 < ±72) → tablet can spawn while visible on a wide screen. Vanilla also only checks INTERSECTION with the rect (so the box is centered on the player center, ±72/±40.5).

Hmm, for a smaller window (e.g., 1280×720): vanilla box = 96×54 tiles → ±48×±27. Ours ±50×±40 → ours blocks MORE. So the delta depends on resolution. The comment says "玩家 50 格内视为可见(WorldEvolution.playerLOS 同款近似)" — it's an approximation, resolution-dependent. Worth reporting as a documented-but-imprecise approximation, noting vanilla is a 1.2×screen rect, and that a resolution-derived box would be exact. Medium-low severity (visual pop-in risk). Both cultist tablet and fairies use it.

Also note vanilla PlayerLOS iterates ALL players (any active player) — ours only this.player (single-player assumption). Fine for this repo.

442	    const st = this.world.store;
443	    this.spawnTileX = spawnTileX; this.spawnTileY = spawnTileY; this.spawnTileType = spawnTileType;
444	    // waterTile（L957）：落脚格上方两格都是液体且为水
445	    const above1 = st.idx(spawnTileX, spawnTileY - 1), above2 = st.idx(spawnTileX, spawnTileY - 2);
446	    this.waterTile = st.liquid[above1] > 0 && st.liquid[above2] > 0 && st.liquidType[above1] === 1;
447	    // nearMarble/nearGranite（L960-1046）：落点 tile → 玩家 tile → 两轮邻近扫描
448	    // (落点 ±Next(20,30) 步长 Next(1,4) + 玩家 ±Next(30,60) 步长 Next(3,7) 抽样)。

450	    if (spawnTileType === T.MARBLE) this.nearMarble = true;
451	    else if (spawnTileType === T.GRANITE) this.nearGranite = true;
452	    else if (st.type[st.idx(this.pX, this.pY)] === T.MARBLE) this.nearMarble = true;
453	    else if (st.type[st.idx(this.pX, this.pY)] === T.GRANITE) this.nearGranite = true;
454	    else {
455	      const scanType = (cx0: number, cy0: number, r: number, stepI: number, stepJ: number) => {
456	        const x0 = Math.max(0, cx0 - r), x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, cx0 + r);
457	        const y0 = Math.max(0, cy0 - r), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, cy0 + r);
458	        for (let i = x0; i <= x1; i += stepI) {
459	          for (let j = y0; j <= y1; j += stepJ) {
460	            const tt = st.type[st.idx(i, j)];
461	            if (tt === T.MARBLE) this.nearMarble = true;
462	            else if (tt === T.GRANITE) this.nearGranite = true;
463	          }
464	        }
465	      };
466	      scanType(spawnTileX, spawnTileY, rng.int(20, 30), rng.int(1, 3), 1 + (rng.next() * 3 | 0));
467	      scanType(this.pX, this.pY, rng.int(30, 60), rng.int(3, 6), 3 + (rng.next() * 4 | 0));
468	    }

   *  若此处误用缩放后的 def.damage 会与命中端乘区叠加成双重缩放 */
===EM add===
export class EntityManager {
  nextId = 1;
  enemies: Entity[] = [];
  drops: Entity[] = [];
  npcs: Entity[] = [];
  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）

  add(e: Entity, bucket: 'enemies' | 'drops' | 'npcs' | 'projectiles' | 'props' | 'critters') {
    e.id = this.nextId++;
    this[bucket].push(e);
  }

  update(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 就地压缩替代 6×filter(2026-08 审计 G9:每 tick 6 个新数组,60Hz 恒定分配)
    for (const list of [this.enemies, this.drops, this.npcs, this.projectiles, this.props, this.critters]) {
      for (const e of list) e.fixedUpdate(dt, game);
      compact(list);
    }
  }

  clear() {
    this.enemies.length = 0;
    this.drops.length = 0;
    this.npcs.length = 0;
    this.projectiles.length = 0;
    this.props.length = 0;
    this.critters.length = 0;
  }

  all(): Entity[] {
    return [...this.enemies, ...this.drops, ...this.npcs, ...this.projectiles, ...this.props, ...this.critters];
  }
}

```

```
**Two important findings**:

**A. Main.rand is NOT reseeded per frame** — it's created once at startup (Main.cs:6413-6415, `if (rand == null) rand = new UnifiedRandom((int)DateTime.Now.Ticks)`) or in DedServ. So Main.rand is a persistent stream consumed by the whole game (including setFireFlyChance and every spawn roll). Our Math.random replacement loses stream determinism/correlation but preserves roll ORDER. Combined with `WorldGen.genRand => Main.rand`, vanilla has ONE persistent stream. Our Math.random is a documented approximation — for runtime behavior it's statistically equivalent but not seed-reproducible. Informational, already documented in the header (though the header's "two streams" claim is wrong for 1456).

**B. `NPC.Spawner.fairyLog` has a real consumer: `CheckToSpawnUndergroundFairy`** (NPC.cs:5731+): if fairyLog (any fallen log exists in the world), there's a 1/500 (1/250 anniversary, ×1.66 hardmode) RollLuck chance per spawn attempt to spawn an UNDERGROUND FAIRY near the player (presumably 583-585). Our scan does NOT set any fairyLog equivalent, so this underground fairy spawn path is missing. Let me read the rest of CheckToSpawnUndergroundFairy to see what it spawns and where it's called from. This is a genuine gap: the batch implemented MysticLogFairiesEvent but missed the companion `Spawner.fairyLog` flag + CheckToSpawnUndergroundFairy. Let me read the full function and its call site.

All anchors confirmed. Now I have everything I need to write the final report.

Let me organize the findings by severity:

## Findings (by severity)

### F1 (Medium) — FallenLogDestroyed rescan runs before the tile is actually removed
- File: ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Game.ts:5749-5751 (top of `breakTile`)
- Vanilla: WorldGen.cs:49863-49869 — `if (type == 488) mysticLogsEvent.FallenLogDestroyed();` sits at the END of KillTile, after `destroyObject=false` + the 5×4 TileFrame refresh loop, i.e., after the tile is gone.
- Ours: the hook is the first thing in breakTile, before every removal path (tree/pot/larva/plantera/track... all early-return branches come later).
- Failure: player breaks a fallen log at night → rescan sees the tile still active → stump stays in `_stumpCoords` until the next StartNight or another log breaks → fairies can spawn at the destroyed log's (now empty) coordinates for the rest of the night. Vanilla excludes it immediately.
- Fix: move the hook to the end of breakTile's removal path (or schedule the rescan after `st.setTile(x,y,0)`).

### F2 (Medium) — fireFlyFriendly / fireFlyMultiple are rolled + persisted but have zero consumers
- RuntimeEvents.ts:53-63 rolls them; World.ts:85-87 + serialize/SaveFile/SaveClient/worldPacket persist them; grep shows no consumer anywhere.
- World.ts:81-82 comment claims "fireFlyFriendly/fireFlyMultiple → spawnFriendly 夜萤火虫群(NPC.cs:2321/:2329)" — false.
- Vanilla consumers: NPC.cs:2321-2345 (night friendly-path firefly 355/358 + 4 extras @ 1/fireFlyMultiple), NPC.cs:4421-4433 (night enemy-path extras after the 1/fireFlyChance gate — our VanillaSpawner.ts:2180 returns a single 355/358 with no extras), NPC.cs:5768-5780 (SpawnLavaBaitCritters night lavafly 654 clusters — our VanillaSpawner.ts:2287 delivers 1, comment acknowledges).
- Fix: either wire the consumers or drop the two fields from persistence; at minimum fix the World.ts comment.

### F3 (Medium) — Underground fairy spawn chain missing (Spawner.fairyLog + CheckToSpawnUndergroundFairy)
- Vanilla: MysticLogFairiesEvent.ScanWholeOverworldForLogs sets `NPC.Spawner.fairyLog = true` when any log exists (MysticLogFairiesEvent.cs:181-183); consumed by NPC.Spawner.CheckToSpawnUndergroundFairy (NPC.cs:5730-5757: fairyLog + RollLuck(500/250 anniv ×1.66 hardmode) + depth window [(worldSurface+rockLayer)/2, maxTilesY-300) + !AnyHelpfulFairies), called from the spawn chain at NPC.cs:3523 → spawns 583-585.
- Ours: RuntimeEvents scan doesn't set any equivalent flag; no underground fairy branch in VanillaSpawner.
- Effect: fairies only ever appear at night near surface logs; the underground fairy path never fires.

### F4 (Medium-Low) — freeCake dialogue priority inverted vs vanilla (and contradicts its own comment)
- Game.ts:9272-9278 (inside townNpcChat): freeCake check comes AFTER `npcSpecialEventText` (HasSpecialEventText).
- Vanilla: NPC.cs:94974-94978 — GetChat's very first statement is `if (nextDialogue != null) return nextDialogue.GetChatAndClearCondition(this);` (nextDialogue refreshed every frame at NPC.cs:91579). Cake line beats every other line.
- Code comment at 9268 claims "GetChat 头部 nextDialogue 先行".
- Effect: during a party + blood moon/rain/etc., vanilla shows the cake line; ours shows the event line and defers the cake (and item) to a later talk.
- Also minor: vanilla delivers the cake via `QuickSpawnItem(EntitySource_Gift)` (drops as item); ours does `player.inv.add` directly. Documented-ish, cosmetic.
- Fix: move the freeCake block to the top of townNpcChat (before npcSpecialEventText).

### F5 (Low-Medium) — placeBoundRescueNpcs' vanilla claim is wrong; worldgen bound placement has no vanilla counterpart
- Game.ts:1873-1911 places bound goblin/mechanic/stylist/golfer/tax collector/wizard for every NEW world (wizard only hardmode).
- Vanilla: WorldGen places ONLY the guide (or seed-specific NPCs) at worldgen — WorldGen.cs:19980-20041; the ONLY vanilla spawn path for 105/106/685 is the SpawnAnNPC chain NPC.cs:1997-2008 (with downedGoblins/hardMode/downedBoss3 gates + RollLuck(20)); mechanic/stylist/golfer/tax collector likewise spawn only via their chains.
- Effect: day-1 bound goblin tinkerer without ever fighting a goblin invasion, etc.; and the trio chain at VanillaSpawner.ts:1636-1649 is effectively unreachable for 105/106 in new worlds (only 685 relies on it).
- (This also answers the prompt's unique-gate question: no misjudgment — `saved*` flags and AnyNPCs are separate, placeBoundNpc doesn't set saved flags, and freeBoundNpc:1973-1990 sets them only on rescue.)

### F6 (Low) — cultistDelay discarded on vanilla .wld import
- WldParser.ts:184 `if (version >= 108) r.i32(); // cultistDelay` — value skipped, unlike neighbors (sundialCooldown:168, fastForwardTimeToDawn:194 are captured). Vanilla WF:2185-2192 reads it for v108+.
- Effect: importing a vanilla world resets the tablet respawn delay to the 86400 default even if it had partially elapsed. One-line fix (capture into the parser output).

### F7 (Low) — setFireFlyChance: no load-time re-roll + non-vanilla defaults
- Vanilla re-rolls the five values at EVERY world load: WorldFile.cs:770 `NPC.setFireFlyChance()` (in the load callback, after liquid settle), plus every dusk (Main.cs:64707). The NPC statics default to 0 (NPC.cs:6036-6044) and are never in WorldFile.
- Ours: rolled only at DUSK (Game.ts:3239); defaults 30/5/15/10/999999 (World.ts:85-89, serialize.ts:327).
- Effect: (a) mid-day save/load keeps the same night's values where vanilla re-rolls; (b) new-world first day uses fixed representative values instead of a fresh roll. Documented as intentional persistence, but the WF:770 load-time roll is missing from the batch's notes — worth either adding a load-time re-roll (vanilla-faithful) or a comment.

### F8 (Low) — PlayerLOS approximation is resolution-dependent and can under-block
- Game.ts:9905-9908 / 9933-9936: |Δx|<50 && |Δy|<40 tiles.
- Vanilla WorldGen.cs:69500-69515: point 16×16 rect vs player-centered rect of sWidth*1.2 × sHeight*1.2 (screen-size based; ~±72×±40 tiles at 1920×1080).
- Effect: on wide screens ours blocks less than vanilla → tablet/fairy can pop into view; on small windows it over-blocks. Direction: conservative on small screens, permissive on large. Suggest deriving the box from camera view size ×1.2.

### F9 (Low) — anyDanger approximation drops DangerThatPreventsOtherDangers
- Game.ts:9901-9903. Vanilla NPC.cs:81040-81084 also treats {517,422,507,493,399,13,14,15} (NPCID.cs:4869) as danger: the four Celestial Pillars, Moon Lord core, EoW body/tail.
- Effect: during the Lunar pillar phase (lunarEvent.active, countdown 0) our anyDanger=false → cultist recheck = 600 instead of 3600 → tablet respawn attempts continue during the apocalypse (vanilla pauses them). Same for EoW body/tail if our 14/15 defs aren't boss-flagged.
- Comment in RuntimeEvents.ts acknowledges the gap.

### F10 (Low) — duplicate id assignment in spawnTablet/spawnFairy
- Game.ts:9939-9944 (`e.id = this.entities.nextId++; this.entities.add(e, ...)`) and spawnFairy (same pattern) — EntityManager.add (Entity.ts:81-84) already does `e.id = this.nextId++`. The manual assignment is dead and burns an id each spawn (id gaps only, no functional break).

### F11 (Informational) — DUSK block order deviates from vanilla Main.cs UpdateTime_StartNight
- Vanilla: ResetBadgerHatTime(:64703) → freeCake=false(:64704) → Star.NightSetup(:64705) → setFireFlyChance(:64707) → BirthdayParty.CheckNight(:64708) → LanternNight.CheckNight(:64709) → mysticLogsEvent.StartNight(:64710) → ... → eclipse clear(:64721-22).
- Ours (3229-3257): badger → freeCake → setFireFlyChance → mysticLogs.startNight → eclipse → LanternNight.checkNight → partyCheckNight. mysticLogs before the two CheckNights, party after lantern, eclipse clear before.
- No shared state between these systems and all rolls are Math.random → functionally equivalent. Cosmetic only.

### F12 (Informational) — UpdateTime subsystem order
- Vanilla Main.cs:64425-64434: time += dayRate; CultistRitual.UpdateTime → BirthdayParty → LanternNight → Sandstorm → DD2 → CreditsRoll → mysticLogsEvent.UpdateTime → PylonSystem.
- Ours Game.ts:2942-2965: LanternNight.updateTime → cultist edge → cultistRitualUpdateTime → mysticLogs.updateTime. Independent systems; equivalent.

### F13 (Informational/comment) — RuntimeEvents header RNG claim is wrong for 1456
- RuntimeEvents.ts:19-20 claims "Main.rand / WorldGen.genRand 两条 RNG 流 → 单一 Math.random 流". In 1456 `WorldGen.genRand => Main.rand` (WorldGen.cs:4391) — ONE stream; Main.rand is created once at startup (Main.cs:6413-6415), NOT reseeded per frame. So our single Math.random stream with vanilla roll order is statistically faithful; only seed-reproducibility is lost. Comment correction.

### F14 (Comment stale) — bossAI_lunar_misc.ts:~452
- `// CultistRitual.TabletDestroyed()(复活延迟账)——事件延迟系统未引入,略` — now stale: the account IS kept, via the Game-side ai0==-1 edge capture (Game.ts:2950-2957). The capture itself is correct: our tablet AI sets ai0=-1 in the same block where vanilla calls TabletDestroyed (NPC.cs:38841-38881), and vanilla has no other call site, so no missed path (the tablet is dontTakeDamage in vanilla — there is no "player breaks the tablet without summoning" path to miss).

### F15 (Informational) — RollLuck(20) vs N(20) in the trio gates
- Vanilla trio gates use RollLuck(20) (luck-scaled); VanillaSpawner.ts:1638-1647 uses flat N(20). Luck integration absent. Minor; consistent with other spawner approximations.

Also worth noting the answer to "会不会漏掉石碑先被玩家打掉(未召出 439)的路径" — NO (covered in F14).

And the "五路" verification (all OK) + the F6 sixth path.

And the firefly night gate verified 1:1 including the 358-only-for-109 mapping (mowed hallowed 492 → 355 in both).

## 已核实无误清单

1. **setFireFlyChance 三档分布** (RuntimeEvents.ts:50-80 vs NPC.cs:94560-94610): all value ranges (5-9/1-3/3-7, 10-59/2-14/6-29, 999999 sentinels), butterfly/stinkbug mutual exclusion (1/3 + 1/5), tenth-anniversary halving with max(1) — exact 1:1. Roll order preserved.

3. **cultistDelay 五路持久化**: serialize.ts (types + write ??86400 at :328), SaveFile.ts saveGame:71/saveWorldOnly:142/read:260 (`!= null`), SaveClient.ts:64 → save.worker meta spread, protocol.ts:42 + worldPacket.ts:40 + World.fromPacket:326 (`!== undefined`). Default 86400 = vanilla delayStart everywhere including pre-v108 old saves (WF:1018/:2185-2192) — never 0.
4. **MysticLogFairiesEvent** (RuntimeEvents.ts:252-357 vs MysticLogFairiesEvent.cs): WorldClear/StartWorld/StartNight/FallenLogDestroyed/UpdateTime hooks at the right vanilla sites (WorldGen.cs:6958/:3303, Main.cs:64710/:64433, WorldGen.cs:49866); suppression table complete (rain/bloodMoon/MoonLordCountdown/snowMoon/pumpkinMoon/invasion/empty stumps); 3600/10800 ÷60 = 60/180 with moonPhase 0=Full/4=Empty (verified vs Terraria.Enums/MoonPhase.cs + Main.GetMoonPhase:3256-3258); dayRate rolls; stump pick + (x*16+24, y*16+24-50) spawn; count 1-3 + 1/7; per-fairy 585/584/583 + anniversary override; one-batch latch; scan bounds/steps (x 100..w-100 step 3, y worldSurface-10..100 step 2, active && type 488 && liquid 0) + frame-offset stump origin (floor(fx/18), floor(fy/18)); night window [6480.0000965595245, 25920.000386238098] exact literals, Clock fraction math (DAWN .1875/DUSK .8125) verified.
